- **enneagram-analyst**: Lemon Studios Story Editor uses this when analyzing optioned IP, incoming scripts, or books in adaptation to evaluate character psychology and arc potential. Use during IP coverage to assess whether a protagonist has a compelling arc for Latin American audiences, or when diagnosing why a character in development is not working. Triggers on: analyze this screenplay, type these characters, diagnose this character, extract the Enneagram from this script, or evaluate character psychology of optioned material. ([brovzar-lab/lemon-studio-skills](https://github.com/brovzar-lab/lemon-studio-skills/tree/HEAD/enneagram-analyst))
- **enneagram-architect**: Lemon Studios Story Editor uses this when building character systems for original IP or advising on protagonist/antagonist pairing. Use at the start of original development passes when characters are created from scratch, before any pages are written. Head of Development uses this to evaluate whether optioned IP has compelling character opposition. Triggers on: build characters from scratch, design the cast, Enneagram type for my protagonist, position characters for conflict, design the antagonist, or cast design for this story. ([brovzar-lab/lemon-studio-skills](https://github.com/brovzar-lab/lemon-studio-skills/tree/HEAD/enneagram-architect))
- **ennio-morricone-perspective**: 埃尼奥·莫里康内的电影配乐思维框架。基于莫里康内配乐体系， 提炼6个核心心智模型、6条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：作为创作顾问，用莫里康内的视角分析音乐叙事、判断美学方向、评估声音方案。 触发词（符合任一即激活）： - 核心触发：「莫里康内视角」「西部片配乐」「意大利西部」「电影音乐」「无言歌」 - 进阶触发：「极简旋律」「音色实验」「铜管×口哨」「莫里康内公式」「配乐作为角色」 - 场景触发：「黄金三镖客」「美国往事」「天堂电影院」「西西里的美丽传说」「教会」 - 方法论触发：「音乐是第二台词」「极简旋律的力量」「音色即情绪」「无声胜有声」 - 即使用户只是说"这个有没有莫里康内的感觉""帮我用西部片配乐的角度想想""随便聊聊电影配乐"也应触发 ([khanhhuyenngo985-sys/character-scene-design-skills](https://github.com/khanhhuyenngo985-sys/character-scene-design-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/.archive/perspectives/ennio-morricone-perspective) · ★20)
- **ennis-critical-thinking**: Use this skill when the user wants guided Ennis Critical Thinking Abilities applied to real work. ([cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills](https://github.com/cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ennis-critical-thinking))
- **ennoia-build-agent**: Ennoia multi-agent를 새로 만들거나 기존 graph·draft와 모델 설정을 수정, 검증, 테스트, 저장할 때 사용합니다. 이미 배포된 App 실행이나 운영 배포 요청은 별도 Skill의 대상입니다. ([wanteddev/ennoia-plugin](https://github.com/wanteddev/ennoia-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/ennoia/skills/ennoia-build-agent))

- **enola-deepdive**: Use before shaping or planning a change; asks the graph three questions (impact, governing decisions, existing findings) and writes the answers into the plan with the receipt they came from; knowledge-base track, needs the kb wrapper ([misabegovic/enola-guides](https://github.com/misabegovic/enola-guides/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/enola-deepdive) · ★1)
